# State of Continental Trade, Highway Network & `Ria Valley` Hydrology (`5.1e22` — `27` Spheres, `118` Goods)

Generated by [gemini_trade_model.py](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/tools/scale-model/gemini_trade_model.py) over staged `5.1e22` exterior hydrology (`openworld/ria-exterior-522/` via [edit_522_ria_hydrology.py](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/tools/scale-model/edit_522_ria_hydrology.py)), [geology.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/kits/resources/data/geology.json), and [net51-gemini.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/openworld/net51-gemini.json) into [trade-gemini.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/openworld/trade-gemini.json) and [roads-supplement-gemini.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/openworld/roads-supplement-gemini.json).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **27-Sphere Planetary Trade Summary (`Rulings #1–104`)**: **118 trade & workshop I/O goods** (adding `clay`, `fireclay`, `ancient-biogel`, `inferior-biogel`, `superconductors`, `paktong`, and `nephrite-jade`) solved across **63 polities & autonomous enclaves** (`205` real on-map settlements, **`3,349,072` single-counted planetary census** + `120k` metropolitan demand weighting at `Iziz` & `Fulcrum`) over the three-layer directed `4 km` Dijkstra multimodal graph (`Layer 0` Overland Highways & Trails + `Layer 1` Sea & Inland Lakes + `Layer 2` Directed Navigable Rivers & Canals) with `124` supplemental polity, Ria-Dynastic & frontier roads.
> - **`Ria Valley` Sinuous Dendritic Hydrology (`39` Rivers, `sin = 1.29–1.51`) & River-First vs. Medium-Highway Architecture**: Replaces raw `5.1`'s `8` ruler-straight channels (`sin = 1.02–1.12`) with **`39` stream-power-eroded dendritic rivers (`11,300 km` total channel length, mean sinuosity `1.389`)** and the **`x ≈ 702` `"Iron Gates"` hogback water-gap (`4,210 m³/s`)**. The Ria Dynasties maintain a **`0.72×` medium-grade overland highway network** between settlements (`8.8%` of in-basin inland ton-km for lateral feeder & `Weeping Valley` bypass links), while **`9101 Mā-Rīa` + navigable lower `Pāk-Mā`/`Mō-Mā` forks carry `79.3%` of in-basin inland freight ton-km** as Ria's primary bulk highway!
> - **Twin Ria-Based Trans-National Organizations Pushing South ([NORTHERN-CARTELS-SCOPE.md](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/core/region/NORTHERN-CARTELS-SCOPE.md), `Rulings #102–104`)**: Strictly neutral above both Ria Dynasties (`Springhollows` `1135,153`, `"Iron Gates"` `702,164`, `Ria Sound` `626,140`), **(1) *The Pioneers / The Technicians*** (`PN-01..15`) export `Lodestone` `superconductors`, clean **hydro-dynamos** (`electrics`), silk-wrapped telegraph relays, and anomaly-proof flux-compasses (`lenses`) down twin southward pincers (`Silkhead -> Port Koron/Shell -> Dilihan/Skywatch -> Talia -> Iziz/Hook` and `Ria Sound -> Battered Isle -> Farport -> Roketstad/Slussport -> Ys/Port Mira`), rivaling southern Geomancer `oil` while secretly mapping Crater passes & `RC` Relic flux-fields; **(2) *The Court of the Amber Seal*** ("The Seal-Bank", `AS-01..12`) acts as Krator's true trans-national sovereign bond, Ghost-Tsunami/*bottomry* cargo insurer, and customs/lock-farming bank.
> - **Continental Corridors & Wave-0 Locks**: Includes the **Northern Lifeline** (`Shell -> Port Koron 912,532 -> Soda Lake lbl=54 -> Silkhead 934,424 -> Lodestone`), **throttled `Cascade–Drum` `89 km` bore** (partially collapsed at game start, `Ruling #57`), **`Lake lbl=77` bi-national scow/barge compact** (`Jimjam <-> Palewharf`), **`Verge` `856 m` Geomancer oil-barrel cableway** (`Ruling #45`), **Dual `Yusar` salvage/cybernetics corridors** (`Span` Shadow-Trail + `Tone`), and the **Annual Great Caravan** via `P8` `The Marches` (`Ruling #40`).

## 0. `Ria Valley` Hydrology & River-vs-Highway Modal Split (`edit_522_ria_hydrology.py`)

| Metric | Value | Notes |
|:---|---:|:---|
| **Ria + Shroudhills Dendritic Rivers** | **`39`** (`+32` new 1st–3rd order branches) | Up from `7–8` ruler-straight lines; `11,299.8 km` total channel across `842,384 km²` (`13.41 km / 1,000 km²`) |
| **Channel Sinuosity (`9101 Mā-Rīa` / `9107 Pāk-Mā` / `9108 Mō-Mā`)** | **`1.290` / `1.368` / `1.380`** | `32` new tributaries span **`1.300 – 1.510`** (mean **`1.396`**) with stream-power valley erosion & meander benches |
| **`"Iron Gates"` Transverse Hogback Sill (`x ≈ 685–718`)** | **`+1,725 m` crest / `4,210 m³/s` gap** | `101.6 km` navigable water-gap (`slope = 0.00045`); puts `Heartland` (`580 mm`) & `Moleshelf` (`549 mm`) in `BSk` rain-shadow |
| **In-Basin Inland Modal Split: River Barges (`Layer 2`)** | **`79.3%` of inland ton-km** | `2,311,864.3` ton-km — **`Mā-Rīa` & lower `Pāk-Mā`/`Mō-Mā` are Ria's primary bulk highway** |
| **In-Basin Inland Modal Split: `0.72×` Ria Medium Highways** | **`8.8%` of inland ton-km** | `255,070.1` ton-km — inter-settlement post-roads, `Lodestone` feeder & `Moleshelf` bypass |
| **In-Basin Inland Modal Split: Frontier Pack/Caravan Trails** | **`11.9%` of inland ton-km** | `348,023.3` ton-km — `Spider Lake` / `Silkhead` & high mountain rim approaches |

## 4. Complete Good-by-Good Atlas (All 118 Goods)

### I. Metals, `Paktong`, Compass Magnets, Bullion & Structural/Alloy Salvage

#### `iron` — Iron & Steel (`bulk`, unit: `t`)
- **Role**: mined iron ore & smelted bar; heavy bulk cargo feeding weapons, armor, firearms & tools
- **Cross-Polity Volume**: **`634.0 t/yr`** · **Pirate Capture**: `9.401 t` · **Mode Split**: Ship `38.8%` | River/Canal `26.7%` | Highway `28.7%` | Caravan `1.9%` | Tunnel/Pass `4.0%`
- **Producers (Exporters)**: **Eternal State** (`232.18` exp / `15680.03` surp, 31.4%), **Iron Republic** (`30.42` exp / `5623.87` surp, 11.3%), **Vothic Kingdom** (`19.07` exp / `3768.44` surp, 7.5%), **Talovolci Chiefs** (`25.61` exp / `3550.97` surp, 7.1%), **Yuni Emirate** (`4.23` exp / `3280.18` surp, 6.6%), **Realm of Dalab** (`29.88` exp / `2401.51` surp, 4.8%)
- **Importers (Customers)**: **Lower Ria Dynasty** (`201.33` imp / `201.33` need, 31.7%), **Tabisti: Sfenops** (`78.96` imp / `78.96` need, 12.4%), **Yonni & Shroudhills** (`60.37` imp / `60.37` need, 9.5%), **League: Ys** (`42.73` imp / `41.29` need, 6.5%), **Moleshelf Clans** (`40.34` imp / `40.34` need, 6.4%), **Silkhead (Korona Gateway)** (`36.13` imp / `36.96` need, 5.8%)

| Flow (`Origin -> Destination`) | Origin Polity -> Buyer Polity | Volume | Dist (`eff km`) | Mode | Ports, Passes & Corridors Traversed |
|:---|:---|---:|---:|:---|:---|
| **Fulcrum -> Heartland** | Eternal State -> Lower Ria Dynasty | `61.216 t` | 2237 km (`546.1`) | `ship` | ports: Farport -> Ria Sound; via Farport -> Ria Sound |
| **Fulcrum -> Ria Sound** | Eternal State -> Lower Ria Dynasty | `51.119 t` | 1711 km (`365.1`) | `ship` | ports: Farport -> Ria Sound; via Farport |
| **Fulcrum -> Yonni** | Eternal State -> Yonni & Shroudhills | `38.101 t` | 859 km (`231.2`) | `ship` | ports: Farport -> Yonni; via Farport |
| **Reed -> Sfenops** | Nomad Country -> Tabisti: Sfenops | `30.898 t` | 28 km (`33.1`) | `highway` | direct trail |
| **Reed upriver fields (to be named) -> Sfenops** | Nomad Country -> Tabisti: Sfenops | `27.414 t` | 59 km (`75.5`) | `highway` | Desert Nomad Bandits |
| **Noa's Regret -> Port Mira** | Ring Sea Pirates -> Hookfleet | `1.165 t` | 102 km (`59.9`) | `ship` | PIRATE PRIZE FENCE; ports: Noa's Regret -> Port Mira |

#### `copper` — Copper (`medium`, unit: `t`)
- **Role**: bronze base, roofing, wire, circuit boards, coin & distillery pot-stills
- **Cross-Polity Volume**: **`439.68 t/yr`** · **Pirate Capture**: `24.586 t` · **Mode Split**: Ship `31.7%` | River/Canal `14.5%` | Highway `34.1%` | Caravan `6.7%` | Tunnel/Pass `13.0%`
- **Producers (Exporters)**: **Xanadu Sultanate** (`16.08` exp / `1124.75` surp, 18.0%), **Vothic Kingdom** (`44.55` exp / `634.12` surp, 10.2%), **Realm of Dalab** (`31.92` exp / `529.85` surp, 8.5%), **Tungang Union** (`33.69` exp / `510.61` surp, 8.2%), **Zeijani** (`41.34` exp / `492.75` surp, 7.9%), **West Ring Islanders** (`50.06` exp / `379.33` surp, 6.1%)
- **Importers (Customers)**: **Eternal State** (`190.52` imp / `199.69` need, 45.2%), **Yusar (Cybernetics)** (`75.16` imp / `78.78` need, 17.8%), **Empire of Iziz** (`87.08` imp / `74.67` need, 16.9%), **Iron Republic** (`24.89` imp / `26.04` need, 5.9%), **Lower Ria Dynasty** (`10.32` imp / `10.74` need, 2.4%), **Jabal Shiekdom** (`8.82` imp / `9.31` need, 2.1%)

| Flow (`Origin -> Destination`) | Origin Polity -> Buyer Polity | Volume | Dist (`eff km`) | Mode | Ports, Passes & Corridors Traversed |
|:---|:---|---:|---:|:---|:---|
| **Zey'danin -> Fulcrum** | Vothic Kingdom -> Eternal State | `12.89 t` | 1242 km (`817.5`) | `highway` | ports: Zey'danin -> Tethis; Iron Republic Nauen Lake-Barge (Slussport-Roketstad); Iziz-Voth Naval War (Shipping Raids); Scyvoi Lowland Raids |
| **Nulab -> Fulcrum** | Realm of Dalab -> Eternal State | `10.781 t` | 1818 km (`1035.9`) | `train/pass` | Loj Fower Cliff Lifts (Ledge-Nulab) + Tungang South Bore; Fellgang Raider Risk |
| **Ledge -> Fulcrum** | Tungang Union -> Eternal State | `9.852 t` | 1758 km (`981.9`) | `train/pass` | Tungang South Bore + Tungang Deep Hot Bore (>100 C) |
